Stage 4 then needs six passes through trigger 34, a 2.2 by 2.2 column (x -0.69 to 1.51, z 1042.41 to 1044.61, from height 6.8 up, centre 0.405, 1043.506): the trigger refuses the hero, so a Harris must walk into it; each pass removes the Harris that fired it and the count rises by 1. | 722 on | 723 on (count 6); region 1401 (the Whinnies) loads | H: either. SW: none. AR: inside. Resolved (R-0223; Medium: computed from the enemy rules, not played): the firer is one of the Evil Harrises themselves (the module tests entities 16 to 21 against the trigger volumes every tick), so no hero action fires trigger 34: it refuses every hero contact, shot and blow, and a hero standing in the column blocks a Harris's fire as well. The column is 4.2 by 4.2 for a Harris (x -1.69 to 2.50, z 1041.41 to 1045.60 at its body radius 1.0), tall enough that the Harrises' feet height of 9.6 is inside it. The Harrises start at x +30.4 and -30.6, z 1076.7, 1051.7 and 1024.3, height 9.6, wandering. Plan for an automated player: (1) walk into the arena (the arm) and let the Harrises notice the hero; once one fights, all six come; (2) face one Harris and keep it between the hero and the column's centre on one straight line, at most about 14 units from the column, then either run at it from under 7 units so that it backs straight away (15.9 units per second) into the column, or strike it once with the sword from about 2.5 units so that it slides about 14.6 units straight away from the hero; stay out of the column; (3) a pass is about 11 to 14 s of scene; the firer is removed for good and the other five are put on the ring of points round the arena; repeat with the next Harris until the count is 6 (six Harrises, six passes). Do not leave the shrine area before the count is 6: the removals can be remembered while the count restarts at 0. A Harris's own swing is refused for the firing test, and it swings only when the hero faces away, so face it. Worked sequences are in SPEC-0043 (stage 4). Default if unresolved (rewrite choice): as above; if no pass happens within 180 s, move to 20 units from the nearest Harris and try again; report blocked after 600 s without a new pass. The Drackonacks of region 1399 take no damage in this pack; do not fight them. L |

Resolved (Medium-High; ground and walls, not played): the plaza floor is one connected floor, not pieces. The pack's own ground has a base floor at height 129.64 (x -72 to 112, z -72 to 168; the mosaic well at x 24 to 56, z 32 to 64 is a second patch at 129.63) that is part of the ground wherever no other patch lies above it; earlier work lost both patches because their outline lists are empty and an entry with an empty outline list always passes the cell's list test (SPEC-0018, Ground probe, step 3), which is why the lower plaza looked enclosed. On that floor the lower plaza (records 0, 2 and 4, on mounds at height 134.6 with edges at 130) rises to the terraces by ramps of about 0.25 slope (units of height per unit of length): the upper terraces (heights 142 to 163) hold the three mosaic pieces and the exit rectangle of goal 520. Walks from record 0 (-8.0, 136.9, 48.0) with the walls of the pack (ledge climbs up to 6.7 allowed): item 25 at (56, -62) is 163 units, all walking, via (-7, 19), (2, -11, 133.8), (23, -23, 140.5), (53, -26, 142.6) and (53, -56, 149.6); item 26 at (83, 115) is 210 units, all walking, via (-7, 79), (2, 109, 134.2), (23, 118, 140.5), (50, 103, 142.6), (68, 79, 145.6) and (80, 97, 150.8); item 27 at (-98, -18) is 208 units with two ledge climbs and one fall of 3, via (-22, -8), (-25, -17, 140.1), (-55, -8, 145.7), (-73, 1) and (-88, 10, 150.9); the mosaic cylinder (40, 48) is 48 units; the east terrace (records 1 and 5 at x 80) is 270 units (one ledge climb near (89, 100) and one drop); the exit rectangle at (90.6, 41.9) is 277 units. The terrace south of the wall line at z 165.5 (to z 196, height 149.6) is cut off from the plaza in the model by that one-sided wall line (a U-shaped enclosure open to the north) and a second blocked line at z 175, so it is not part of the route. Pack 1411's cells there name pack 1391 (every one of its 833 cells), so the hero leaves the pack on the ground of pack 1391 wherever the pack's own ground ends. The lift patch of pack 1391 (height 0.07, x -36 to 36, z 188 to 252) is joined to no other floor of pack 1391, so the hero reaches it by walking south (+z) off the upper terrace at x about 0, a drop of 149.5 (SPEC-0049 gives the same drop). | none | pack 1391 current | H: either. The two placed objects with floor data are object 0 (a 1.4 by 13.7 strip at x 68.5 to 69.9, z 41 to 55, which the mosaic scene plays and Init leaves at its raised pose when flag 334 is set; it rises 19.88 from 153.6, below the terrace surface of 159.6 there, to 173.5) and object 1 (a 7 by 19 patch at (-87.7, 145.7, 48), drawn by Init until trigger 17's scene hides it). The free width is never below 15 units (SPEC-0054, A7). Gaps settled in this list.
- Heroes: the shot meter of goal 508 needs Roberta (only she fires hero shots). The final fight is Tor for the three stuns and Roberta for the three shots and the landing, switched by script under swap mode 3; the player cannot switch in the arena. Pack 1394's four levers switch the hero by script in the order Roberta, Tor, Roberta, Tor, whichever order the levers are used in.
- Items 25, 26 and 27 are world pickups on the upper terraces of pack 1411 (goals 516 to 518). The six elemental Motes needed for the pedestals are world pickups in packs 1411, 1363, 1350 and 1358 (goal 562).
- Pack 1371 branch B (the wave trial) is entered from teleport 2 of pack 1350, not through the arena master; its exit counts as a won arena round.
- The Altador statue puzzles: pack 1353 needs 2, 6 and 7 presses; pack 1397 needs 0, 3, 2 and 1 presses.
- The pack 1394 lever course needs no particular lever order; each lever switches the hero by script.
- Pack 1382 is reached from pack 1391 by the lift and the volume that sets the next world; pack 1381 is optional (Resolved: no code outside pack 1381 reads flags 708 to 719, and the lift is entered from pack 1411).
- Resolved: after movie 54 the hero stands at record 1 of pack 1300 (value 50 for world 151). The way to Queen Fyora's chamber is not on foot from the garden (pack 1300 cannot swap, and its door to pack 1335 is locked because flag 92 is set for good). It is 1300 teleport 2 to 1327, 1327 door 1 to 1299, the 1299 pad staircase and its bottom teleport to 1335 (flag 248 on), then a walk over the floor of 1335 to z -74 on x 0, pack 1344's flat floor (height 7.4) past two doors that open on contact (flag 322 on) and pack 1428's corridor at (0, -270) (goals 501 and 502; High for the doors and flags, Medium for the walks).
- Resolved: Altador is one x-z frame. The hub plateau (height 30) at the origin drops by spiral stairs to the Arena (north), Bazaar (east) and Money (west) floors, and the park is joined to the plateau on the south; the waypoints are in goals 521, 522, 530, 536 and 550.
- Resolved: the Coliseum waves are wave 1 (region 1378): one Brute, three Warriors, two Grunts; wave 2 (region 1379): one Brute, one Warrior, two Wizards, two Magicians; wave 3 (region 1380): three Brutes, three Wizards, and up to four Grunts that Wizards lob.
- Resolved: the Mortog yard's zone centre is locator 6, (0.0, -0.7, 361.7), the plate's own point; locator 7 gives the radius 5.2.
- Resolved: the stage-4 firer in pack 1398 is an Evil Harris, never the hero (the Whinnies are not loaded while stage 4 is armed).

Open items and defaults.
- Resolved: the route from the Palace Gardens to Queen Fyora's chambers (goals 501 and 502) and the Altador hub routes (goals 521, 522, 530, 536, 550) are given as floor waypoints. Resolved: a world swap has no height test, but pack 1300 is on the no-swap list, so there is no crossing from the garden to pack 1344; the original route runs through the scripted doors 1300 to 1327, 1327 to 1299 and 1299 to 1335 (flag 248 on) and then over the ground 1335 to 1344 to 1428 (High for the doors and flags; Medium for the walks). Still open: the climb to the Treasury door slab in pack 1358 (re-checked with walls, ledge climbs and jumps and with the outline rule corrected: the slab, its arched strip, a small bridge piece at x -503 to -494 (height 22) and the elevated roads (x -489 to -252, heights 21.6 to 22.6) are one elevated network joined by hops of 2 and 6 units, so the old gap of 17 was an error; but the whole network is an island, the nearest ground of the hub stair at the same height is about 185 units away, and nothing in the pack or its script joins it to the ground; Low; Default: path-find to (-528, 0), then try the arched strip beside the slab, and after 60 s blocked place the hero on the slab at (-527.0, 29.56, -2.6), which is inside the upper door's touch window: feet 27.6 to 33.5, within 3.5 of the plane at x -529.6, along z -10.3 to 5.1).
- Resolved in part: the floor route through packs 1392, 1395, 1393 and 1396 into pack 1394 is given (goals 540 to 542, about 1,300 units, one hand-over per pack). Still open (Low): gates, levers and guard rooms (they are not in the floor samples); Default: follow the waypoints, press the nearest unpressed lever when blocked, step on a room plate when a guard door blocks. In pack 1394 the floor from record 0 joins only lever S1. Resolved: each lever moves the hero to the next leg's start (records 1, 15, 16, then 8), so the later levers are reached from those spots, not from record 0; the sinking platform has no floor data. Legs A (to S1) and E (record 8 to the exit trigger) are confirmed with walls; leg D (to S4) is walkable with two jumps of 12.1 and 6.3 (Medium-High), leg B (to S3) is a chain of stone hops with one borderline gap of 14.3 to 15.3 (Medium-Low), and leg C (to S2) is not joined by any floor, wall or object data (Low); the crossings are in goals 544 to 546. Default: follow the lever order 15, 16, 18, 17 with jumps up to 15.8 between edges, avoid falls, and report blocked after 90 s; the countdown resets the leg. In pack 1392 the open scenes of the gates sink the corridor platforms under a hero who steps on them (goal 540; Low for how the original avoids it), and the pack 1395 hall is passable on foot (goal 541: the old stop at (-88, -499) was the lattice).

Resolved: the plates raise the lifts, each for one block (plate 11 block 3, plate 10 block 1, plate 9 block 2, used in that order), and all three blocks can reach their plates (3, 47 and 82 moves; goal 534). Still open: the join from the start room to the hall (the pack's own tall one-sided walls close the start room; the ring walkway ends at x 154.2 and the hall's edge starts at x 166.3, a void of about 12 that a running jump crosses; there are no corridors at z +-46; the objects the buttons raise carry no floor data) (Low; Default: pass the vault walls, jump the void from the ring's east end at z 38, and place the hero on the hall edge at (170.0, 16.88, 38.0) after 120 s; goal 534). Resolved: the climb from the lower plaza of pack 1411 to its upper terraces is a walk by ramps (goal 563; the old finding that the plaza was enclosed came from ground patches with empty outline lists that the walk model dropped; Medium-High). The pack 1397 ground has no such patch, so the start-room finding above is unchanged by that correction.

Resolved: of the three wave-3 Wizards only the one with variant 0 (row 211, on the ring point (-0.1, 7.2, -26.5)) lobs Grunts, up to four live at once; the other two (rows 213 and 216) and both wave-2 Wizards never summon (goal 528; High for the one summoner, Medium for the timing); Default: kill the fire Wizard first, then every placed Minion, then every Grunt that appears.

A Harris reaches the column by wandering, by backing away from a hero who closes in, or by a sword knock-back of about 14.6 units; the hero must line up hero, Harris and column and attack or close in (see goal 556). The old default of standing still and not attacking is dropped. Default if unresolved (rewrite choice): the goal 556 plan, blocked after 600 s without a pass.

## Edge cases and error handling

- In a game begun at start index 0, rows whose done flag is never set stay in the journal for the rest of the game once started (some of these flags are set by the stage table for start indices 1 to 8; SPEC-0049): row 145 (done 341) and row 152 (done 352). Rows whose start flag is never set are never listed: row 146 (start 341), row 153 (start 352), row 129 (start 268), rows 117 and 118 (start 323 and 324); pack 1428 sets row 118's done flag 326 anyway. Row 48 (the lunge lesson, flag 182) is never completed. Medium-High (SPEC-0049, Who writes the flags).
- Rows 42 to 46 (five nest rows) share start flag 174 and have done flags 625 to 629. Pack 1056's breakable routine sets them, one flag per nest breakable as each is broken, and advances the 175 to 179 chain (SPEC-0044, 1056), so the rows close as the nests are broken. Medium-High for 625 to 628; Medium for 629 (the fifth breakable).
- The farm counter: each activation of pack 909 clears 107, 108, 109 and 110 whenever any of the four is off. A finished round-up (all four on) is kept; any partial round-up is reset on every entry. High.
- Goals that are free in order or optional (a rewrite must not force the listed order): in the farm, the Whinny round-up (goal 2) and picking up the bucket (goal 4), and the whole pliers chain (goals 10 to 13) until the talk of goal 14; the shield (goal 30) and the three sword parts (goal 35); the four knight records (goals 90 to 93); the nest breakables (goal 71); in Act 2 the picks and the exchange of goals 115 to 117 before goal 118, the two stage-2 blocks (goals 136 and 137), the three bell blocks (goals 139 to 141), and goals 131 and 132 (optional); in Act 3 the Brightvale trip of goal 318 (flag 259 is read only by that pack), the three berries (goals 323, 325, 330, but all before goal 333), the bridge builder (goal 335) and the road guards (goal 336), the Mines talks (goals 356 to 360, whose flags are read only by unreachable code and by an optional interior), the witch (goals 369 and 370), the Neopoints collection (goal 373, any time before goal 405), the grave ritual (goal 382) and the levers of pack 1249 (goal 379), the cloud wall (goal 402, skipped on the fight route), and the Rope Gun farm visit (goal 354, any time after goal 352 and before goal 396); in Act 4 the three mosaic pieces (goals 516 to 518), the six Motes (goal 562, any order and any source), and the districts of Altador (goals 524 to 539) in any order before goal 551. Medium-High.
- Pack 1428 has no once-per-game latch on the trap scene: reloading 1428 with 322 on and touching trigger 4 replays movie 55 and the item swap. Medium.
- The sewer-drain counter in pack 1171 computes its flag as 162 + n, where n (1 to 5) is the number of drains cleared, so it sets 163 to 167 in turn, including 166 and 167 (rows 37 and 38). High.
- Flags 592 and 593 belong to side-quest rows 228 and 229 (the traitor knight), not to the main story (SPEC-0049).
- Start points (SPEC-0035) set the stage rows but do not move the hero, so a stage start begins in the default packs with the flags of a later point in the story.
- **Thieves' crew in 1139 (R-0148; High for the script and the idle rule, Medium-High for the design reading).** Pack 1139 places five characters, in entity order 0 to 4: Heermedjet (the thief of the hand-over; monster type 39, variant 1), Meerouladen (type 39, variant 0), Gil the Fence (type 72, row 152), Drool (type 72, row 152) and Lump (type 71, row 151). The last three are class-17 rows with the hostile word set, but none is meant to be fought. (1) What keeps them idle: the placement's default AI mode word is 0 on all five (no path, no wander size, alarm group byte 255, no drop, not summon-reserved). In class-17 state 0 the per-tick step only re-applies that default word, and the notice check (the row's sense band, awareness value and view cone) runs only in states 3, 5, 6, 7 and 9. So the three never notice the hero at any range and their agro range is never evaluated. No script, entity call or alarm moves them: the 1139 module makes no AI, agro, walk, scene, camera or death call on any entity. Only a hit moves one into the agro state; it then fights (row 152: 50 hit points, strafe approach; row 151: 60 hit points, rush approach) and no story step reacts. Thieves of the same rows elsewhere use default modes 5, 7 or 8. (2) What stops the hero: no wall, placed object or movable block does. Pack 1139 has no movable block, its one placed object stands at the origin, and the nearest collision shape to each body centre is 8.4 units for Drool (a cylinder), 5.7 for Gil (a cylinder) and 4.2 for Lump (a post of radius 1.24 at (51.1, -9.9)). The solid thing is each character's own body: an upright cylinder of the monster row's radius (2.0 for rows 151 and 152), against the hero's 1.175. The shared body separation (SPEC-0033, bodies pushing the hero) pushes the hero out to a centre separation of about 3.1 to 3.2 (radius sum 3.175), not 4.5. A hero held at about 4.5 is held by something the original does not have (the body sum would have to be 4.5, for example a hero radius of 2.5). (3) Talk: a class-17 character is not on the talker list (townsfolk only), but it is on the lock-on list while alive and not hidden (range 20.0 for Tor, 100.0 for Roberta, facing cosine at least 0.5), and the talk entry is chosen by the candidate's entity id, not by its class. With the entry registered (entities 0 to 4 all are) and the 3-D distance below 5.8, the "Talk" caption shows and Square starts the callback, so the standing range for Gil, Drool and Lump is 3.2 to 5.8 (Medium-High; not played end to end). (4) Script: Init registers the five talk entries and nothing else touches them. Gil (entity 2): the one-time sale of SPEC-0039, then comments by flag. Drool (entity 3): grunts, lines 1 to 3, lines 4 to 6 with flag 305 and lines 7 to 9 with flag 319. Lump (entity 4): wishes of luck with the treasure, lines 37 to 39, lines 40 to 42 with flag 305 and lines 43 to 45 with flag 319. None of the three sets a flag, joins a scene or movie, or starts a later fight; flag 678 is set and tested only by entities 0 and 1. (5) Story: the player has no task with them except to buy from Gil (a potion or a Silver Negg, SPEC-0039) and to hear them; the progress step is Heermedjet. Killing one changes no story state; a dead character is no longer a talk candidate, so Gil's sale would be gone (Medium). Default if unresolved (rewrite choice): build the three as idle talkers in state 0 that never notice the hero, are hittable and enter agro when hit, with body radii 1.175 and 2.0.

## Test vectors

Synthetic, hand-checkable.

1. Act start 3 on an all-off bitmap: flag 248 on, swap mode 2, Tor selected. Rows whose start flag is 248 become listable.
2. Quest rows 0 to 3 (start and done flags 107/108, 108/109, 109/110, 110/111): set 107, and row 0 is listed; set 108, and row 0 is gone and row 1 is listed; set 109, and row 2 is listed; set 110, and row 3 is listed.

4. Pack 1300 entered with entry message 80 after the finale (94 and 365 on): flag 366 turns on and the hero is placed at landing 2. Trigger 2 then plays movie 79 and sets 367; trigger 3 plays movie 83 and sets 368; the next play pass opens the credits, and their end tears the world down.
5. Text lookup (synthetic table): a table block at file offset 0x2000 and a row whose +0x10 field holds 0x0180 give the objective text starting at file offset 0x2180 and running to the first zero byte. A row with scene id 3 names the area whose table-5 row 3 text offset is resolved the same way.
6. Row kind: a row with +0x00 = 1 is a side quest; the story test skips it.
7. Pack 1382 with five pedestals lit: nothing. Light the sixth with a matching Mote equipped: 98 and 337 turn on, movie 70 plays once, and the hero is returned to 1411, where movie 71 can then play (337 on, 338 off). Pedestal check: a Fire Mote (160) on the pedestal at trigger 21 lights it; a Bubble Mote (162) there drops Mote 160 at locator 6.

9. Pack 1327 library door with 240 on, 245 off and 223 already on: 223 unchanged; 245, 246, 247, 400 and 397 on; movie queue [27, 29, 90, 30]; return world 64.

Touching the section rectangles first (triggers 2, 3 and 4) changes no story flag.

After Square on the Items tab (the active hero now wears item 126) and no attack or hit before the talk, the payment is offered; yes removes 1,000 Neopoints and sets 13.

## Open questions

Questions 1 to 14 have resolved story-route answers, with the remaining geometry and reachability caveats stated in each entry. Question 12 retains a reachability part. The clean side must not fill open gaps by inspecting the original.

1. Resolved: flag 337 (the step that wakes Jerdana is done) is set, with flag 98, in pack 1382 once all six elemental pedestals are lit; each is lit by a weapon strike with a Mote of its element equipped, lit pedestals stay lit for the rest of the load, and the order is free (SPEC-0041). Movie 70 plays and the hero returns to 1411. The chain is 1411 -> 1391 -> 1381 -> 1391 -> 1382 -> 1411, then movies 71 and 72, then the final fight in 1432 (step 46; SPEC-0049, Who writes the flags). The two 1381 groups (flags 708 to 719) are described in SPEC-0042: a station strike sets 708 + k, and movable block k pushed onto its pad spot sets 714 + k. The load from 1391 into 1382 does not wait on them in code: 1391's trigger 1 needs only the hero's contact (High). Resolved (computed from the ground data and the placed-object records, not played): the geometry of 1391 does not require the 1381 groups before trigger 1, but the on-foot route through the hub does. Trigger 1 is a box x -40.77 to 39.23, z 180.55 to 260.55, y -229.62 to -69.62 (Medium-High for the order of the three extents), directly under the lift patch (x -39 to 39, z 186.8 to 254.3, y 0.07). The lift is placed object 0 of 1391; it descends in a straight line 218.41 in 19.93 s (10.96 per second), so a hero riding it enters the top of the box 6.36 s after the animation starts, the animation starts 1.5 s after trigger 2 (cylinder base (-0.02, 0.08, 220.55), radius 20, height 0.5) fires, and the lift stops at y -218.34 inside the box. Trigger 1 fires on body contact and needs nothing else (High). Pack 1411 names pack 1391 as a streamed neighbor in cells with exit values 1–4; this is not a fixed arrival point. When the ground probe transfers the current-world role to the streamed neighbor, it checks the hero's same x/z position, with no per-pack coordinate transform, so x and z are shared by 1411, 1391 and 1381. Three ways reach the lift patch without any flag from 708 to 719. (a) On foot through the hub: from 1391's west high ground (joined to the west part of 1411) at (-300, -100, 83.2), then (-238, -166, 68.2), (-172, -232, 49.9), (-106, -242, 35.5), (-18, -242, 16.1) and (-12, -200, 8.0) to the hub floor at (-12, -156, 0.0), south over the flat hub floor (y 0.0) and through the doorway in the hub's south wall (x -7.8 to 8.2 between two wall blocks 20 high, z 75.9 to 90.4) into the corridor (x -24 to 24, z 120 to 190, y 0.0), then (-12, 196, 0.1) and the patch at (-2, 218, 0.1): 814 units, largest rise per 2-unit step 0.52, largest drop 3.4. (b) Around the hub: from the 1391 ridge end (-138, 64, 119.4) an 86-unit fall to the hub's outer ring (-136, 66, 33.3), along the ring (y 33 to 35) to (-28, 160, 35.0) and a 35-unit step-off to the corridor at (-26, 162, 0.0): 457 units. (c) From 1411: the upper terrace is flat at y 149.6 up to z 194 at x 0 and ends before z 197 with no ledge record on its south rim, while the patch begins at z 187, so walking off the rim (z about 195 to 197, x within -39 to 39) is a 149.5 fall onto the patch (falls cause no damage and have no height limit, SPEC-0033 Behavior 9B). Only route (a) meets a closure: the doorway is filled by the gate, a wall box (placed object 19 of 1381, x -10.5 to 11.0, z 81.6 to 84.3, y -1.8 to 35.0), which is switched off, together with object 18 playing its animation, when all six block pieces are done (flags 714 to 719); the six blocks only appear after their stations are solved (flags 708 to 713). What solving changes on the ground: no floor height (the hub, the corridor and the patch stay flat at y 0.0 and 0.07). Each solved station removes its pad bump (a patch about 18 wide with heights -0.7 to 1.45 at (0, -47.4), (41.0, -23.8), (41.5, 23.8), (0.0, 47.6), (-41.1, 23.5) and (-41.0, -23.9)) and brings block k into the hub at (0.0, -25.9), (22.4, -12.8), (22.5, 12.8), (0.0, 25.9), (-22.4, 13.0) and (-22.4, -13.0) (5.5 wide, 12.0 tall for blocks 0 and 5, 9.6 for blocks 1 to 4); the hero pushes each onto its goal point (0.2, -45.9), (39.8, -22.9), (39.6, 23.0), (-0.1, 45.9), (-39.7, 22.8) or (-39.8, -23.0), and the last block opens the doorway. Confidence: High for the box, the lift numbers and the ground values; Medium that the gate is solid from the load (its wall records have positive run lengths until it is switched off) and that the doorway is the only low link between the hub and the corridor (the wall faces of the hub are not all modelled); Medium-Low for the two fall routes at run time (the world hand-over during a fall). Default if unresolved (rewrite choice): build the gate as on the disc (solid until flags 714 to 719 are all set) and keep the falls legal; add no other requirement before trigger 1. Packs 1411, 1391 and 1381 each have an empty type-10 table, so none provides an authored ladder or climb panel for this route (SPEC-0006, Type 10); this does not rule out other movement or collision paths.
2. Resolved except three flags: every main-story flag the story tests has an ordinary level-script setter (SPEC-0049, Who writes the flags): 106, 356 and 724 in 1398 (all Whinnies on their plates, movie 67); 342 to 344 in 1371 (waves 6 to 8); 127, 128 to 135 and 136 in 1023 (the Meerca chase start, waypoints and catch); 158 in 1163; 197 in 842; 200 in 800 (Ixi Raider Chieftain defeated); 233 in 1327 (the end of the rapid-fire step of target practice; 234 marks the target destroyed); 366 in 1300; 708 to 719 in 1381. The exceptions: 341 has no script setter and no script reads it; the journal reads it, and the stage table sets it (stage 7) for some start indices 1 to 8, so in a game begun at start index 0 it stays off (journal only, like 352 and 268, which are also stage-table flags); 182 has no live script setter, because its only script setter is in a routine nothing calls, so in a game begun at start index 0 the lunge stage flag 25 is never set and the squire chain skips the lunge lesson (High; SPEC-0044, 1056; SPEC-0049 for the start indices that set 182); 167 has no fixed-number setter but is computed as 162 + n by the 1171 clog counter.
3. Resolved: the doors of pack 722 (Bogshot Village) enter the interiors 745, 748, 749, 750 and 752, so the apothecary (745) and the bridge builder's house (752) are reached (steps 32 and 34). Pack 722 itself is reached through cells (Medium).

Pack 1394 has a script exit back to pack 1353 (world 158), opened by its trigger 11 (it sets 104 and 353; flag 0 is not needed), so the shrine is not a dead end (High).
5. Resolved: the door from 1363 into the Petpet shrine 1398 opens when four Mortogs are on their targets in 1363 (flag 692 is set then and the door opens in the same visit; on later visits it opens at entry from 692). Flag 355 is only the second condition.
6. Resolved: flags 342, 343 and 344 are set by the 1371 wave controller when waves 6, 7 and 8 are cleared. Pack 1371 is a five-round arena entered from 1350 (SPEC-0041, SPEC-0042). Flag 341 is journal-only (question 2).
7. Resolved (High): 777 is reached by a script load from 758 and 1017 by a script load from 991. Pack 1077 is an unused copy of pack 1129, whose module it shares; nothing loads it, and it stays one of SPEC-0006's 17 unreached packs. Step 41 (the hand-over and flag 305) runs in 1139 and then 1129.
8. Resolved: pack 1023's Meerca chase sets 127 at the start (the package, item 32, is taken; flag 7 also set), 128 to 135 at the waypoints and 136 at the catch (the package is given back, movie 4).
9. Resolved: pack 1300 sets flag 366 when entered with entry message 80, which the finale sends on its return (step 46, Endings), so the epilogue movies 79 and 83 can play.
10. Resolved (High): the Act 1 opening in pack 909 needs 707 on and 1 off. A second block of the same update, with 1 on and 706 off, repeats only the fade, camera and placement, so no single test names flag 1 both on and off (step 2). The act start is applied before movie 88 is shown. The Act 3 start in pack 1327 needs 240 on and 245 off; flags 400, 223 and 397 are each set only while off and are not terms of the gate (step 27).
11. Resolved (High for the script match, Medium-High for the entity order): fight instance 1 (pack 985, flag 281 clear) is Gnarfas; instance 5 (pack 985, flag 281 set) is the Gelert Assassin; instance 12 (pack 1428) is the trap in Queen Fyora's chambers, with Queen Fyora and the Darkest Faerie, ended by script (steps 19, 39 and 44).
12. Resolved (High): trigger firing. The trigger fire step marks a script trigger record as entered: state 1 on the first fire, then 2 while it keeps firing on later ticks; the script step decays a stale state (no fire for more than one tick) to 3 and then back to 0. Every script trigger that starts a main story step fires by the selected hero's own body contact (object 1), with two exceptions: the hit count of 1428 trigger 5 counts hero shots, and 1398 trigger 34 refuses hero fires and is fired by an entity through CollideEntityWithTriggers. No story trigger is unreachable. Pack 1174 trigger 1 (step 21) is a static rectangle fired by the hero's body contact or by a hero shot. Who fires what, where and when: SPEC-0028, Behavior, Trigger pass.
13. Resolved: movie 59 never plays in play (High). Flag 333 is set by pack 1428's act-start sequence before the hero first reaches pack 1411 (world 178), and by the Act 4 start index; no level script clears 333. Pack 1411 trigger 0 plays movie 59 only while 333 is off, so no order of play reaches it. Movie 60 and the mosaic (334) are not affected. Clean behaviour: reproduce the gate as stored; movie 59 stays unplayed.
14. Resolved for script order (High), narrowed for physical access (Medium): in pack 777 (step 30), all five triggers are registered on every load. Index 4 fires on contact, once per load, while 253 is off; it does not test 252, so it can fire before movie 35, and its 252 then silences trigger index 0 (movie 35 and flag 69 skipped). Indices 5 to 8 each need Square and their own flag off; the code sets no order among them, but 256 (index 8) stops the routine and the exit stays shut until 253 to 256 are all on. Static placement data put index 8 on a pedestal similar to the other book pedestals, with ground patches at its top and no library-table object at or around it; the module has no flag gate that requires 253 to 255 first. The lowest ground patch on the pedestal is 14.5 units above the dais top (47 versus 32.5). The documented hero jump peaks about 3.20 units above takeoff, so a direct jump from the dais cannot reach that patch. Pack 777 has three type-10 ladder groups (six records, all ladder-face/top records) and no climb-panel records; the nearest projected horizontal record segment is about 68.43 units from the index-8 trigger center. This rules out a ladder or climb panel beside the middle pedestal, but does not exclude an indirect route through other geometry or interaction from below. Ground-patch and climb records do not establish collision geometry, climbability, or continuous navigation. Therefore no static script or placement support forces the intended book order, while actual first-use reachability remains unconfirmed (SPEC-0033, Behavior 50 and vector 117; SPEC-0006, Type 10; SPEC-0045, pack 777).
15. Resolved (computed from the ground data, not played): Rope Gun route requirement. The pack-909 grant is gated by the Free Tor's Farm progress flag (6), by item absence and by the Farm-return message of pack 1217's exit plane (row 39 gives the scene order). In the Glade, the item-36 test selects dialogue only; targets 0–2 are activated in both possession branches, and no script checks that a rope was fired. Physically the rope is required: the dais at the lift bottom (y 4.38) is reached from the lower ground by two ledge hangs of 6.6, and the climb from the dais to the shaft-top plateau (y 67.7, 63.35 higher, one middle platform at y 27.1) and from there to the glade slab has no other way (smallest gap 35.8 or more against a ledge reach of about 6.95; lift off in Act 3). Triggers 11, 12 and 15 therefore need the three shots of goal 397, and trigger 10 (flags 281 and 66) comes first, from the dais. High for the gaps, Medium that no object or wall route exists that the ground data does not show. Only the Farm-return scene gives the item, so the route needs that scene to have run. Default if unresolved (rewrite choice): make the rope mandatory as above.
16. Resolved (R-0148; High for the script and the idle rule, Medium-High for the design reading): Gil the Fence, Drool and Lump in pack 1139 are not fights. They are class-17 rows placed in default mode 0, so they never notice the hero, and the 1139 module only registers talk entries for them. The hero stops at about 3.2 from their centres by body radii (1.175 and 2.0), not at 4.5, and no wall, object or movable block stands near them. Details and the default: Edge cases, thieves' crew in 1139. Still open: why a rewrite build holds the hero at about 4.5 is not explained by disc data.
17. Resolved (High; module conditions checked on the story route): (a) Pack 1098's story-stage helper is not a module word: it returns the highest of flags 31 down to 21 that is set, or none. "Stage 30" means flag 30 on and flag 31 off (flag 31 is set by the Act 3 start). (b) Pack 1217's fight-due test is flag 83 on with 278 off, or flag 83 off with 145 on and 82 off; its phase conditions test bits of one module bit word (file value 0) that only the fight controller sets (SPEC-0045, 1217 Stage word), so they hold on the Act 1 and Act 3 routes from the start value. A visit with 83 on and 278 on runs no fight. (c) Pack 1371's wave flags 342, 343 and 344 are set by constant-flag branches on the wave word, in that order, and 345 by trigger index 1 (SPEC-0041, 1371). (d) The caves levers of 1129 gate objects, not flags in other packs (goals 410 to 412).
